{{Short description|none}}
{{Use dmy dates|date=July 2024}}
{{2024 Summer Olympics}}
This is a chronological summary of the major events of the [[2024 Summer Olympics]] in [[Paris]] and other venues in [[Metropolitan France]], plus one subsite in [[Tahiti]] in the [[Overseas country of France|overseas country]] of [[French Polynesia]]. Competition began on 24 July with the first matches in the group stages of [[Football at the 2024 Summer Olympics|football]] and [[Rugby sevens at the 2024 Summer Olympics|rugby sevens]] events. The [[2024 Summer Olympics opening ceremony|opening ceremony]] was held two days later on 26 July. The last day of competition and the [[2024 Summer Olympics closing ceremony|closing ceremony]] was held on 11 August.<ref>{{cite web |last=Butler |first=Nick |date=7 February 2018 |title=Paris 2024 to start week earlier than planned after IOC approve date change |url=https://www.insidethegames.biz/articles/1061155/paris-2024-to-start-week-earlier-than-planned-after-ioc-approve-date-change |url-status=live |archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20201109023013/https://www.insidethegames.biz/articles/1061155/paris-2024-to-start-week-earlier-than-planned-after-ioc-approve-date-change |archive-date=9 November 2020 |access-date=7 February 2018 |website=Inside the Games}}</ref>
 
The games included 329 events in 32 different sports. [[Breaking at the Summer Olympics|Breakdancing]] made its debut as an Olympic event.<ref>{{cite web |last=Keicha |first=Meshack |date=19 December 2020 |title=Kenya To Send Break Dancers To Paris For 2024 Olympic Games |url=https://boxscorenews.com/kenya-to-send-break-dancers-to-paris-for-olympic-games-p158469-272.htm |access-date=31 March 2023 |website=Boxscore |language=en-US |archive-date=1 April 2023 |archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20230401031746/https://boxscorenews.com/kenya-to-send-break-dancers-to-paris-for-olympic-games-p158469-272.htm |url-status=dead}}</ref>

==Calendar==
Competition began two days before the opening ceremony on 26 July, and ended on 11 August 2024.<ref>{{Cite web |title=OLYMPIC SCHEDULE |url=https://olympics.com/en/paris-2024/schedule |archive-date=12 March 2024|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20240312145204/https://olympics.com/en/paris-2024/schedule|url-status=live}}</ref>

On 29{{nbsp}}July, the weather in Tahiti turned dangerous towards the end of round{{nbsp}}3 of the [[Surfing at the 2024 Summer Olympics – Men's shortboard|men's shortboard event]], forcing the cancellation of the [[Surfing at the 2024 Summer Olympics – Women's shortboard|women's third round]]. The waves continued to be too rough for competition over the next two days, leaving the remaining of the surfing finals moved to 5 August.<ref name="p24-surfing-weather-cancelled">{{cite web |title=Surfing: Action called OFF for today |url=https://olympics.com/en/paris-2024/live-updates/12d7e009-3124-4e5b-931c-107a83fb2f87 |website=Paris 2024 |access-date=31 July 2024 |date=31 July 2024 |archive-date=1 August 2024 |archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20240801135641/https://olympics.com/en/paris-2024/live-updates/12d7e009-3124-4e5b-931c-107a83fb2f87 |url-status=live }}</ref>

==Day-by-day summaries==
===24 July===
;[[Football at the 2024 Summer Olympics|Football]]
* The first day of group stage matches of the [[Football at the 2024 Summer Olympics – Men's tournament|men's tournament]].<ref>{{cite news | url = https://www.lemonde.fr/en/sports/article/2024/07/25/paris-2024-olympic-men-s-football-matchday-1-roundup_6697507_9.html | title = Paris 2024: Olympic men's football matchday 1 roundup | date = 24 July 2024 | accessdate = 28 July 2024 | agency = [[Le Monde]] | archive-date = 27 July 2024 | archive-url = https://web.archive.org/web/20240727072547/https://www.lemonde.fr/en/sports/article/2024/07/25/paris-2024-olympic-men-s-football-matchday-1-roundup_6697507_9.html | url-status = live }}</ref>

;[[Rugby sevens at the 2024 Summer Olympics|Rugby sevens]]
* The first day of pool stage matches of the [[Rugby sevens at the 2024 Summer Olympics – Men's tournament|men's tournament]].<ref>{{cite news | url = https://apnews.com/article/olympics-2024-rugby-sevens-92735fdd418cef3142fe29058dab92cd | title = France struggles in Day 1 of the Olympic rugby sevens tournament | date = 24 July 2024 | accessdate = 28 July 2024 | work = [[Associated Press News]] | archive-date = 28 July 2024 | archive-url = https://web.archive.org/web/20240728225231/https://apnews.com/article/olympics-2024-rugby-sevens-92735fdd418cef3142fe29058dab92cd | url-status = live }}</ref>

===25 July===
;[[Archery at the 2024 Summer Olympics|Archery]]
* The ranking rounds of both the [[Archery at the 2024 Summer Olympics – Men's individual|men's individual]] and [[Archery at the 2024 Summer Olympics – Women's individual|women's individual]] events.<ref>{{cite web | url = https://www.reuters.com/sports/olympics/archery-lim-shoots-world-record-signal-korean-intentions-2024-07-25/ | title = Archery-Lim shoots world record to signal Korean intentions | first = Lori | last = Ewing | date = 25 July 2024 | accessdate = 28 July 2024 | work = [[Reuters]] }}</ref> 

;[[Football at the 2024 Summer Olympics|Football]]
* The first matches in the group stage of the [[Football at the 2024 Summer Olympics – Women's tournament|women's tournament]].<ref>{{cite news | url = https://apnews.com/article/olympics-2024-womens-soccer-spain-canada-japan-new-zealand-f0808ce9d20efa211a2e6909e0d8178a | title = Swanson's 2 goals lead US over Zambia at the Olympics, Canada tops New Zealand after drone scandal | first = Anne | last = Peterson | date = 25 July 2024 | accessdate = 28 July 2024 | work = [[Associated Press News]] | archive-date = 27 July 2024 | archive-url = https://web.archive.org/web/20240727230937/https://apnews.com/article/olympics-2024-womens-soccer-spain-canada-japan-new-zealand-f0808ce9d20efa211a2e6909e0d8178a | url-status = live }}</ref>

;[[Handball at the 2024 Summer Olympics|Handball]]
* The first matches in the group stage of the [[Handball at the 2024 Summer Olympics – Women's tournament|women's tournament]].<ref>{{cite web | url = https://www.euronews.com/my-europe/2024/07/26/paris-olympics-latest-france-wave-of-success-continues-in-football-handball-and-rugby-seve | title = Paris Olympics latest: France wave of success continues in football, handball and rugby sevens | first = Alessio | last = Dell'Anna | date = 26 July 2024 | accessdate = 28 July 2024 | work = [[Euronews]] | archive-date = 29 July 2024 | archive-url = https://web.archive.org/web/20240729064154/https://www.euronews.com/my-europe/2024/07/26/paris-olympics-latest-france-wave-of-success-continues-in-football-handball-and-rugby-seve | url-status = live }}</ref>

;[[Rugby sevens at the 2024 Summer Olympics|Rugby sevens]]
* The second and final day of pool stage matches, the first day of placing matches, and the quarter-finals of the [[Rugby sevens at the 2024 Summer Olympics – Men's tournament|men's tournament]].<ref>{{cite web | url = https://apnews.com/article/olympics-rugby-sevens-162491d198e5118fc5e675e38d93658b | title = Unbeaten Fiji on track for third successive Olympic rugby sevens gold by reaching semis | date = 25 July 2024 | accessdate = 28 July 2024 | work = [[Associated Press News]] | archive-date = 27 July 2024 | archive-url = https://web.archive.org/web/20240727151922/https://apnews.com/article/olympics-rugby-sevens-162491d198e5118fc5e675e38d93658b | url-status = live }}</ref>

===26 July===
;[[2024 Summer Olympics opening ceremony|Opening ceremony]]
* The opening ceremony took place outside a traditional stadium setting for the first time, with the [[parade of nations]] conducted as a [[boat parade]] along the [[Seine]] from [[Pont d'Austerlitz]] to [[Pont d'Iéna]], with the official protocol taking place at [[Place du Trocadéro]] in a temporary "mini-stadium". The {{Convert|6|km|miles | adj = on | abbr = off}} parade route featured cultural presentations and views of Paris landmarks.<ref name=":6">{{cite web |last=Ingle |first=Sean |date=13 December 2021 |title=Paris 2024 Olympic organisers reveal audacious plans for opening ceremony |url=https://www.theguardian.com/sport/2021/dec/13/paris-2024-olympic-organisers-reveal-audacious-plans-for-opening-ceremony |url-status=live |archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20220318121115/https://www.theguardian.com/sport/2021/dec/13/paris-2024-olympic-organisers-reveal-audacious-plans-for-opening-ceremony |archive-date=18 March 2022 |access-date=14 December 2021 |website=The Guardian |language=en}}</ref><ref name=":7">{{cite web |date=13 December 2021 |title=Paris to hold 'most accessible' opening ceremony of 2024 Olympics on River Seine |url=https://www.france24.com/en/sport/20211213-paris-to-hold-most-accessible-opening-ceremony-of-2024-olympics-on-river-seine |url-status=live |archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20220417155143/https://www.france24.com/en/sport/20211213-paris-to-hold-most-accessible-opening-ceremony-of-2024-olympics-on-river-seine |archive-date=17 April 2022 |access-date=14 December 2021 |website=France 24 |language=en}}</ref>

===Day 16: 11 August===
;[[2024 Summer Olympics closing ceremony|Closing ceremony]]
* The closing ceremony would be held at [[Stade de France]], at 21:00 local time. It included the traditional [[Olympic flag]] handover to [[Los Angeles]], United States, the host city of the [[2028 Summer Olympics|next Summer Olympics]] in 2028.<ref>{{cite web| url=https://www.hollywoodreporter.com/tv/tv-features/ben-winston-interview-grammys-kardashians-ellen-degeneres-special-1235922168/|title=Producer Ben Winston Talks Grammys, 'Kardashians' Future and Ellen DeGeneres Special| website=[[The Hollywood Reporter]] |date=15 June 2024 |url-status=live |archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20240615143124/https://www.hollywoodreporter.com/tv/tv-features/ben-winston-interview-grammys-kardashians-ellen-degeneres-special-1235922168/|archive-date=15 June 2024 |access-date=15 June 2024}}</ref>
